White's options to get out of check:
1. Capture the piece giving check: Can White capture g7? No pieces can reach g7.
2. Block the check: This is a pawn check (adjacent), so blocking is impossible.
3. Move the king:
Legal moves for King at h8:
- g8: Is g8 safe?
  - Ra8 attacks g8 if Ke8 weren't there. But Ke8 is at f8. So Ra8 doesn't attack g8.
  - The pawn at g7 attacks f8 and h8. It does NOT attack g8.

  - King at f8. Can f8 reach g8? Yes, it's adjacent.
  So g8 is attacked by the Black King. Moving to g8 is illegal.

  - Ke8? No.
  - Qa6? No. (It's on the 6th rank).
  - Pa7? No.

  So h7 is safe.
- g7: Is g7 safe?
  - Can the White King capture g7?
  - If the king captures g7, is g7 defended?
  - The Black King is at f8. f8 is adjacent to g7.
  - So Pg7 is defended by the Black King.
  - Therefore, capturing g7 (h8xg7) is illegal.
